### [94] [A Finite-Lattice Model from a Reciprocal Cost Action: Spectral and Reflection-Positivity Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07922)
*Jonathan Washburn,Megan Simons*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维立方晶格上以双曲余弦势为键作用的统计力学模型。对于连续非紧模型，由于自然时间核的傅里叶变换出现负值，无法通过Bochner正定检验建立反射正性；而对于有限字母变体（场值限制在对称有限集），当关联Toeplitz矩阵半正定时反射正性成立。对于特定参数，通过对角占优准则证明了均匀的反射正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究d'Alembert泛函方程在正则性和标定假设下选择的最近邻键势为倒数成本$J(e^\varepsilon)=\cosh\varepsilon-1$的有限格点统计力学模型，旨在探讨该模型的反射正性（reflection positivity）成立条件及其局限性。

Method: 严格数学分析：对连续非紧模型，通过区间认证的数值积分方法证明了时间核$K(u)=\exp[-(\cosh u-1)]$的傅里叶变换在特定频率处为负，从而阻碍了标准的Bochner-Osterwalder-Schrader反射正性路径；对有限字母变体，通过构造有限交叉键Toeplitz矩阵及其对角占优准则，证明了特定参数下反射正性成立。

Result: （1）连续非紧模型：时间核的傅里叶变换$\widetilde K(3)<0$，不满足Bochner正定条件，因此标准反射正性路径失效；（2）有限字母变体：对于$v_0\in\{1.2,1.5,2.5\}$，通过严格的对角占优证明，反射正性在$N$一致意义下成立，对应的一步转移算子是正的自伴算子。

Conclusion: 该有限体积结果不足以构建连续Wightman理论、Osterwalder-Schrader重构、LSZ散射或连续质量间隙。连续非紧模型的反射正性受阻，而有限字母变体在特定参数下可实现反射正性，但两者均无法直接推广到连续量子场论框架。

Abstract: We study the finite-lattice statistical-mechanical model whose nearest-neighbor bond potential is the reciprocal cost $J(e^\varepsilon)=\cosh\varepsilon-1$, selected by the d'Alembert functional equation under the stated regularity and calibration assumptions. The structural inputs are stated explicitly; once they are fixed, the analysis is rigorous mathematics about the bond action $V(Δφ)=\cosh(Δφ)-1$ on finite boxes in $\mathbb Z^3\times\mathbb Z/8\mathbb Z$. Our main result pairs a negative and a positive statement about reflection positivity. For the continuous noncompact model the natural temporal kernel $K(u)=\exp[-(\cosh u-1)]$ fails the Bochner positive-definiteness test: an interval-certified quadrature gives $\widetilde K(3)<0$. Thus the standard Bochner route to Osterwalder-Schrader reflection positivity is obstructed. For a finite-alphabet variant, with field values restricted to a finite symmetric set $Φ=v_0\{-N,\ldots,N\}$, reflection positivity holds whenever the finite crossing-bond Toeplitz matrix $(K_{Φ(v_0,N)})_{a,b}:=K(b-a), a,b\inΦ$, is positive semidefinite. For $v_0\in\{1.2,1.5,2.5\}$, this is discharged by a rigorous diagonal-dominance certificate uniform in $N$, and the associated one-step transfer operator is then positive and self-adjoint in an explicit reflection-positivity inner product. These finite-volume results do not provide a continuum Wightman theory, Osterwalder-Schrader reconstruction, LSZ scattering, or a continuum mass gap.

</details>


### [95] [Tracking metastable phases by complex Lee-Yang zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08004)
*Yi-Hua Dong,Ling Liu,Fang-Cheng Wang,Qi-Jun Ye,Xin-Zheng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Lee-Yang零点理论，证明了亚稳相存在于热场复平面中，并展示了人工参数或驱动强度增加时，亚稳相如何从复平面逼近实轴并稳定存在，为理解非平衡态提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚稳相是能量上不利的状态，通常在平衡相图中被抑制而“隐藏”。作者旨在揭示亚稳相实际存在于复平面热场中，并通过Lee-Yang零点进行刻画。

Method: 使用可调态密度的玩具模型（三高斯峰）和更现实的周期驱动系统，通过数值计算Lee-Yang零点的位置和分支行为，研究人工参数或驱动振幅变化时亚稳相的涌现与稳定化。

Result: 在两种模型中，随着人工参数或驱动振幅增加，界定亚稳相的Lee-Yang零点从复平面趋近实轴并分裂成独立分支，标志着亚稳相在相邻稳定相之间的扩大间隙中涌现并稳定。驱动系统中，Lee-Yang零点的虚部与驱动强度相关。

Conclusion: 该发现提供了描述亚稳相的方案，将周期驱动视为复热场，为非平衡集体态的理解和工程化提供了新视角，并建立了Lee-Yang理论与太赫兹物质操控之间的联系。

Abstract: Metastable phases (MPs) are energetically unfavorable states typically suppressed in equilibrium phase diagrams. Rather than remaining ''hidden'', we show that they exist in the complex plane of thermal fields, as regions delineated by Lee-Yang zeros (LYZs). We demonstrate this numerically in a toy model with a tunable density of states featuring three Gaussian peaks and in a more realistic periodically driven system. In both cases, as artificial parameters or drive amplitudes increase, the LYZs bounding the MP approach the real axis and split into separated branches, signaling the emergence and stabilization of the MP within the enlarged gap between two adjacent stable phases. In the driven system, the imaginary part of LYZs correlates with drive strength, linking Lee-Yang theory to terahertz matter manipulation. These findings provide a scheme to describe MPs in phase diagram analysis. By viewing periodic drives as complex thermal fields, it also offers a new perspective for understanding and engineering non-equilibrium collective states.

</details>


### [96] [Enhanced dumbbell diffusion in a periodic potential by the elevator effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08334)
*B. A. Kiang,H. Schiessel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过分子动力学模拟研究哑铃（两个珠子由弹簧连接）在一维周期势中的随机游走，发现当哑铃的平衡距离与势周期不匹配且弹簧常数足够大时，会出现增强扩散现象，扩散常数可超过单珠子的值，称为“电梯效应”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究哑铃状结构（两珠子通过弹簧连接）在一维周期势中的扩散行为，特别是当系统参数（弹簧常数、平衡距离、势振幅）变化时，哑铃的扩散特性如何偏离单珠子的经典结果。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟，研究哑铃（两个珠子由弹簧连接）在一维周期势中的随机游走，并与极限情况下的理论解析方程进行对比分析。

Result: 哑铃的平衡距离与势周期不匹配且弹簧常数足够大时，扩散得到增强，扩散常数可超过单珠子在相同势场中的扩散常数。

Conclusion: 哑铃表现出类似“电梯效应”的增强扩散机制，其中两个连接的珠子分别充当电梯轿厢和对重，协同克服周期势垒。

Abstract: We present molecular dynamics simulations of the random walk of a dumbbell - two beads connected by a spring - in a one-dimensional periodic potential and compare our results in limiting cases to theoretical analytical equations. The relevant parameters in this system are the spring constant, the equilibrium distance of the spring (relative to the periodicity of the potential), and the amplitude of the potential. Dumbbells with equilibrium distances incommensurate with the potential periodicity and with a sufficiently large spring constant exhibit enhanced diffusion. The diffusion constant can exceed that of a single bead in the same potential landscape. In this case, the dumbbell resembles a traction elevator, with the two connected beads acting as the elevator car and counterweight: the ''elevator effect''.

</details>


### [97] [A spectral model of power-law decay in natural and engineered systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08342)
*Balázs Sándor,Márk Honti,Henrique Santos Lima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理光谱机制，揭示了非理想输运系统中非广延$q$-指数稀释和幂律弛豫的涌现原因。通过将不完全混合反应器建模为具有吸收边界的层状扩散矩阵，发现宏观幂律尾依赖于初始示踪剂位置与边界构型的几何相互作用。一维系统中非对称体积分布初始浓度投影到低波数本征模，产生弛豫率的伽马分布；在边界层厚度趋近零时，该分布精确给出$q=5/3$的非广延$q$-指数衰减函数。扩展到$d$维时，缩放指数和$q$值依赖于吸收边界的空间构型，但在$d=1$极限下，$q=5/3$指数具有几何不变性。该工作建立了线性扩散输运与非广延统计力学之间的清晰联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非理想输运系统中幂律弛豫和非广延统计行为的微观物理机制，建立线性扩散输运与非广延统计力学之间的理论联系。

Method: 将不完全混合反应器建模为具有吸收边界的层状扩散矩阵，通过光谱分析研究初始浓度分布与边界几何构型对弛豫动力学的影响。

Result: 一维系统中非对称体积分布初始浓度产生弛豫率的伽马分布，在边界层厚度趋近零时精确给出$q=5/3$的非广延$q$-指数衰减；$d$维情况下缩放指数和$q$依赖边界空间构型；$d=1$时$q=5/3$具有几何不变性。

Conclusion: 建立了线性扩散输运与非广延统计力学之间的清晰连接，证明重尾输运行为可从边界几何和光谱维度推导得出。

Abstract: We present a first-principles spectral mechanism for the emergence of nonextensive $q$-exponential dilution and power-law relaxation in non-ideal transport systems. By modeling an incompletely mixed reactor as a layered diffusion matrix with an absorbing boundary, we demonstrate that macroscopic power-law tails depend on the geometric interaction between the initial tracer placement and the domain's boundary configuration. For a one-dimensional system, an asymmetric, volumetrically distributed initial concentration profile projects onto the low-wavenumber eigenmodes, generating an emergent Gamma distribution of relaxation rates; at an infinitesimal boundary layer thickness ($Δz \to 0$), this profile yields the nonextensive $q$-exponential decay function exactly across the entire temporal domain with $q = 5/3$. Extended to $d$ dimensions under a highly localized, boundary-adjacent singular initial condition, the resulting scaling exponents and corresponding $q$ values depend explicitly on the spatial configuration of the absorbing boundaries. However, in the one-dimensional limit ($d=1$), these distinct initial states and boundary formulations intersect, rendering the $q=5/3$ exponent geometrically invariant. Our approach establishes a clear connection between linear diffusion transport and nonextensive statistical mechanics, showing how heavy-tailed transport can be derived from boundary geometry and spectral dimensionality.

</details>


### [98] [What Is a Pattern in Statistical Mechanics? Formalizing Structure and Patterns in One-Dimensional Spin Lattice Models with Computational Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08509)
*Omar Aguilar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用信息论和计算力学方法，形式化定义了三种一维自旋晶格模型（有限程伊辛模型、固上固模型和三体模型）的结构与模式，推导了玻尔兹曼分布，并将其重构为随机过程，通过计算力学中的超额熵、统计复杂度和epsilon-机器进行分析，结果与统计力学典型构型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将计算力学框架（超额熵、统计复杂度、epsilon-机器）系统性地应用于一维自旋晶格模型，形式化定义其结构与模式，并验证与统计力学的兼容性。

Method: 首先推导嵌入无限构型中的有限一维自旋构型的玻尔兹曼分布；然后将该分布重构为随机过程，利用计算力学理论分析模型；通过超额熵量化可预测信息，统计复杂度量化存储信息，epsilon-机器刻画结构生成机制；最后与玻尔兹曼分布的典型构型进行比较验证。

Result: 信息测度和epsilon-机器联合确定的构型与从玻尔兹曼分布中抽样的典型构型一致，验证了计算力学框架与统计力学的兼容性。

Conclusion: 计算力学方法可有效形式化一维自旋晶格模型的结构与模式，且与统计力学结果相容，为后续将计算力学推广到更复杂物理系统提供了基础。

Abstract: This work formalizes the notions of structure and pattern for three distinct one-dimensional spin-lattice models (finite-range Ising, solid-on-solid, and three-body), using information-theoretic and computation-theoretic methods. We begin by presenting a novel derivation of the Boltzmann distribution for finite one-dimensional spin configurations embedded in infinite ones. We next recast this distribution as a stochastic process, thereby enabling us to analyze each spin-lattice model within the theory of computational mechanics. In this framework, the process's structure is quantified by excess entropy (predictable information) and statistical complexity (stored information), and the process's structure-generating mechanism is specified by its epsilon-machine. To assess compatibility with statistical mechanics, we compare the configurations jointly determined by the information measures and epsilon-machines to typical configurations drawn from the Boltzmann distribution, and we find agreement. We also include a self-contained primer on computational mechanics and provide code implementing the information measures and spin-model distributions.

</details>


### [99] [Wave Resistance for Stochastic Motion at Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08732)
*Maxence Arutkin,Shlomi Reuveni,Elie Raphael*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究随机轨迹下波浪阻力的平均效应，发现有限阻力存在于确定性辐射阈值以下，并正则化了毛细-重力波最小相速度处的奇异性。针对漂移布朗运动给出显式标度律，并将理论推广至非高斯的Lévy飞行，得到闭合形式的平均波浪阻力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波浪阻力理论主要针对确定性轨迹，而实际中许多运动（如海洋漂浮物、生物游动）具有随机性。本文旨在理解随机轨迹下平均波浪阻力的行为，特别是阈值以下有限阻力的存在性以及奇异性的正则化机制。

Method: 从流体界面源移动产生的波浪辐射出发，利用系综平均表面轮廓由轨迹历史控制这一事实，推导随机轨迹下的平均波浪阻力。分别针对漂移布朗运动和漂移Lévy飞行两种随机过程，导出显式的标度律和闭合形式解。

Result: 发现随机轨迹下确定性辐射阈值以下存在有限阻力，且毛细-重力波最小相速度处的奇异响应被正则化。对于漂移布朗运动，得到了包含普适高扩散衰减的标度律；对于漂移Lévy飞行，获得了平均波浪阻力的闭合形式表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作将波浪阻力理论从确定性轨迹拓展至随机轨迹，特别是非高斯的Lévy飞行情形，为理解自然界中随机运动物体的波浪阻力提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Wave resistance is the drag generated by the wave radiation that a source moving at a fluid interface sustains. Under stochastic trajectories, the mean drag is controlled by the ensemble-averaged surface profile built from the trajectory history. We show that the result is a finite resistance below the deterministic radiation threshold and a regularization of the singular response at the minimum phase velocity of the capillary-gravity waves. We derive explicit scaling laws for drifted Brownian trajectories, including a universal high-diffusivity decay. For drifted Lévy flight, we find the mean wave resistance in closed-form, extending wave-drag theory to non-Gaussian trajectories.

</details>


### [100] [Fluctuation-stable generalized entropy probes of spectral heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08801)
*Arpita Goswami*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出广义熵诊断的涨落-稳定性框架，从理论上解释了q>1区域具有更好数值稳定性的原因，并通过熵梯度磁化率χ_q有效区分了均匀局域化转变与迁移率边共存 regimes。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义熵测度中q>1区域经验上表现出更好的数值稳定性和清晰的标度行为，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在建立理论框架揭示其物理根源。

Method: 1. 发展涨落-稳定性理论框架，分析不同q值下弱振幅谱涨落的放大与抑制机制；2. 引入热力学标度分析识别渐近稳定阈值；3. 提出熵梯度磁化率χ_q作为谱异质性的粗粒化探针；4. 在Aubry-André与广义Aubry-André模型中进行数值验证。

Result: 证明了q<1时谱涨落被放大、q>1时被抑制，为q>1区域的物理鲁棒性提供了理论基础；χ_q能敏锐区分均匀局域化转变与迁移率边共存两类不同的物理图像。

Conclusion: 涨落稳定性可作为准周期系统中广义熵诊断的指导性原则。

Abstract: Generalized entropy measures are widely used to characterize localization and multifractality, and the regime \(q>1\) is often empirically found to exhibit improved numerical stability and cleaner scaling behavior. Here, we develop a fluctuation-stability framework for generalized entropy diagnostics and show that weak-amplitude spectral fluctuations are amplified for \(q<1\) and suppressed for \(q>1\), thereby providing a theoretical basis for the physically robust \(q>1\) regime. A thermodynamic scaling analysis further identifies an asymptotically stable regime beyond a critical threshold. As an application, we introduce the entropy-gradient susceptibility \(χ_q\) as a coarse-grained probe of spectral heterogeneity. Using the Aubry-André and generalized Aubry-André models, we demonstrate that \(χ_q\) sharply distinguishes homogeneous localization transitions from mobility-edge coexistence regimes. Our results establish fluctuation stability as a guiding principle for generalized entropy diagnostics in quasiperiodic systems.

</details>


### [101] [Time Evolution of Heat Conduction in a Generalized Model of Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08839)
*T. Koide,F. Nicacio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究谐波振荡器网络中的热传导，基于广义布朗运动模型（含动量和位置的噪声与耗散项），推导了稳态热流的解析表达式，验证了傅里叶定律，捕捉了微观热边界电阻，并发现了瞬态热流方向由相互作用性质（吸引/排斥）决定的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准热流定义基于速度在GKSL方程框架下存在数学不一致性，需要建立一个与开放量子系统主方程一致的热力学框架，推动随机热力学与量子热力学的统一表述。

Method: 采用包含动量和位置噪声与耗散项的广义布朗运动模型，基于随机能量学扩展框架推导稳态热流解析表达式，并通过数值求解相关函数的微分方程分析热传导的时间演化。

Result: 1) 该模型满足傅里叶定律（线性热响应）；2) 自然捕捉了类似卡皮查（Kapitza）电阻的微观热边界电阻；3) 广义模型产生连续但处处不可微的动量和位置轨迹（过阻尼动力学特征）；4) 当粒子间相互作用开启时，热流呈现特征性瞬态行为，且方向严格由相互作用是吸引还是排斥决定。

Conclusion: 广义模型可作为模拟非平衡过程的有效唯象框架，是向随机与量子热力学统一表述迈出的关键一步。

Abstract: We investigate the properties of heat conduction in a network of harmonic oscillators interacting with heat baths, described by a generalized model of Brownian motion. This model includes noise and dissipation terms in both the momentum and position equations. This generalization is motivated by the requirement of consistency with the Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) equation. Because standard definitions of heat current based on velocity become mathematically inconsistent in this framework, we derive an analytical expression for the steady-state heat flow based on an extended framework of stochastic energetics. We confirm that Fourier's law (linear thermal response) is satisfied and that the model naturally captures microscopic thermal boundary resistance, analogous to Kapitza resistance. This demonstrates that our generalized model functions as a valid phenomenological framework for simulating non-equilibrium processes, marking a crucial step toward a unified formulation of stochastic and quantum thermodynamics. Furthermore, we analyze the time evolution of heat conduction by numerically solving the corresponding differential equations for the correlation functions. Unlike standard Brownian motion, the generalized model generates continuous and nowhere differentiable trajectories for both momentum and position (as is characteristic of overdamped dynamics). Finally, we show that the heat current exhibits characteristic transient behavior when the inter-particle interaction is switched on. Specifically, an instantaneous heat flow emerges, whose direction is strictly governed by whether the interaction is attractive or repulsive, significantly differing from the predictions of the standard model.

</details>


### [102] [Control transition in a temporally random classical spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09297)
*Elisha Shmalo,J. H. Pixley,Manas Kulkarni,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,David A. Huse*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文理论探索了经典自旋链模型中受控动力学与混沌动力学之间的相变，该模型交替进行混沌哈密顿动力学和收缩性“控制映射”。研究发现，当控制足够强时，目标构型成为全局吸引的稳定不动点；当控制较弱时，多体动力学对几乎所有初态保持混沌。该相变具有混合特征：从混沌相接近转变点时，远离目标构型的自旋分数连续趋于零，存在发散的时空关联长度；但最大李雅普诺夫指数在转变点处不连续跳跃。证据表明该相变与存在时间随机性的有向渗流属于同一普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解经典多体系统中控制与混沌之间的竞争关系，探索由交替的混沌哈密顿动力学和收缩性控制映射驱动的自旋链模型中是否存在受控-混沌相变，并刻画该相变的性质。

Method: 构建了一个经典自旋链模型，其中交替施加混沌哈密顿动力学和将系统驱向目标构型的收缩性控制映射。通过分析不同控制强度下的动力学行为，研究从混沌相到受控相的转变。采用数值和理论方法分析序参量（远离目标构型的自旋分数）、关联长度、李雅普诺夫指数等关键量。

Result: 发现了受控-混沌相变：控制强时系统收敛到目标构型（稳定不动点），控制弱时保持混沌。该相变具有混合特征——序参量连续变化且关联长度发散（类似连续相变），但最大李雅普诺夫指数不连续跳跃。相变普适类与存在时间随机性的有向渗流一致，符合动态Harris判据但不饱和该界限。

Conclusion: 该相变属于有向渗流普适类（含时间随机性），具有连续和间断特征的混合性质，为理解控制与混沌竞争的多体动力学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We theoretically explore a phase transition between controlled and chaotic dynamics in a classical spin chain model subject to chaotic Hamiltonian dynamics and a contractive "control map", which alternate in time. The control map drives the system toward a target configuration that is an unstable fixed point under the chaotic dynamics. When the control is strong enough, the target configuration is the globally attracting stable fixed point of the dynamics; for weaker control, the many-body dynamics remains chaotic for almost all initial states. The phase transition between controlled and chaotic phases has a mixed character: As the transition is approached from the chaotic phase, the fraction of the spins that are far from the target configuration goes continuously to zero, and there are diverging spatial and temporal correlation lengths; however, the leading Lyapunov exponent is discontinuous across the transition, jumping from a positive value in the chaotic phase to a negative value in the controlled phase. We present evidence that this transition is in the same universality class as directed percolation in the presence of temporal randomness, a universality class for which we obtain results that are consistent with the dynamical Harris criterion but do not saturate the bound.

</details>


### [103] [Free fermions in disguise without exponential degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09462)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现了一类新的“伪装自由费米子”（FFD）可解自旋链模型，其谱不存在指数级简并，可通过两个伊辛链的特定微扰或标准Jordan-Wigner可解链与Fendley原始FFD模型之间的插值实现。论文还披露了使用ChatGPT Pro作为研究助手的合作细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中的隐藏自由费米子可解自旋链模型普遍存在能谱指数级简并且均匀分布的问题，本研究旨在构造一个没有此类指数简并的FFD模型。

Method: 通过对两个伊辛链施加特定微扰，或构造标准Jordan-Wigner可解链与Fendley原始FFD模型之间的插值路径，构建新模型；使用ChatGPT Pro辅助研究。

Result: 成功构造了一个FFD可解自旋链模型，在一般耦合常数下能谱不存在指数级简并。

Conclusion: 该模型突破了此前FFD模型必须具有指数简并的认知局限，为隐藏自由费米子可解模型提供了新范例。

Abstract: Recently, a number of spin chain models have been discovered that are solvable via hidden free-fermionic structures, going beyond the Jordan-Wigner paradigm. However, all examples in the literature displayed degeneracies that grow exponentially with the volume and that are homogeneous in the spectrum (identical degeneracies for all energy levels). In this note we present a model that can be solved by ``free fermions in disguise'' (FFD), such that the spectrum is free from exponential degeneracies for generic coupling constants. The model can be seen as a particular perturbation of two Ising chains. Alternatively, it can be realized as an interpolation between a standard Jordan-Wigner solvable chain and the original FFD model of Fendley. We used ChatGPT Pro 5.4 and 5.5 as a research assistant; in the Supplemental Material we provide details about the collaboration between the AI and the human author.

</details>


### [104] [Scaling Behaviors of Work Cumulants in Slow Isothermal Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09552)
*Ruohan Xu,Yanbo Qiao,H. T. Quan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了有能隙系统在慢速等温过程中功的累积量，发现第n阶累积量随过程持续时间T以1/T^{n-1}的标度律变化，且其系数与平衡态热力学几何张量相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解慢速等温过程中功的涨落行为及其标度律，建立非平衡功累积量与平衡态热力学几何量之间的联系。

Method: 采用Martin-Siggia-Rose-De Dominicis-Janssen (MSRDJ) 形式论，结合关联函数的性质进行分析推导。

Result: 证明了对于任意光滑协议，功的第n阶累积量随过程时间T以1/T^{n-1}标度变化；推导出这些累积量的系数可由平衡态性质确定，且与热力学几何张量相关。

Conclusion: 慢速等温过程中功的累积量具有普适的标度行为，其系数蕴含了系统的平衡态几何信息，为非平衡功涨落与平衡态热力学几何之间建立了桥梁。

Abstract: We study the cumulants of work in a slow isothermal process for gapped systems. Using the Martin-Siggia-Rose-De Dominicis-Janssen (MSRDJ) formalism and the properties of connected correlation functions, we show that in this process, the $n$-th cumulant of work scales as $1/T^{n-1}$ , where $T$ is the time duration. This result holds generally for arbitrary smooth protocols. Furthermore, we derive the coefficients of the cumulants from equilibrium properties. These coefficients are found to be relevant to thermodynamic geometric tensors.

</details>


### [105] [Deviations from Debye's specific heat due to excess energy fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09566)
*Ralph V. Chamberlin,Sumiyoshi Abe*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个修正理论，通过时间、相位和热平均的顺序操作来解释晶体中超出德拜T³定律的比热异常，该理论与分子动力学模拟和实验测量结果吻合良好，并为非晶材料和量子器件的异常现象提供了新的理解基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测量的比热经常超过德拜T³定律的预测，即使在高纯度单晶中也存在此现象，而分子动力学模拟显示无缺陷晶体中存在来自次近邻原子间快速能量调制的额外能量涨落，现有理论无法解释这一偏差。

Method: 基于时间平均、相位平均和热平均的顺序平均操作发展理论，该顺序由模拟和实验证据（局域激发与热库解耦）指导，通过反常扩散类比解释非广延性，修正了能量涨落与比热之间的标准关系。

Result: 修正后的理论与模拟结果吻合良好，并为大量实验测量提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 该理论可能为理解非晶材料中的额外比热和量子器件中的异常噪声提供基础。

Abstract: Measured specific heats often exceed Debye's T^3-law, even in high-purity single crystals. Analogous excess energy fluctuations in molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of crystals with no defects come from fast energy modulations involving next-nearest-neighbor atoms. Here, a theory is developed for these modulations, based on time- and phase-averaging followed by thermal averaging. This order of averaging is guided by evidence from the simulations and various experimental techniques showing that localized excitations are decoupled from the heat bath. Emergent nonextensivity is interpreted by analogy with anomalous diffusion. The theory modifies the standard relation between energy fluctuations and specific heat, giving good agreement with the simulations and new insight into many measurements. The theory may also provide a basis for understanding excess specific heat in amorphous materials and anomalous noise in quantum devices.

</details>


### [106] [Geometric Dissipation Constraints in Stochastic Reaction Dynamics: A Variational Observable for Hidden Kinetic Structure in Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09684)
*Shlomo Segal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个几何框架来刻画随机反应动力学中的隐藏动力学约束。通过从Wasserstein空间的梯度流出发，推导出一个变分泛函，其渐近结构定义了局部耗散-几何耦合可观测量。该量结合了力-漂移对齐和相空间压缩，能够区分在传统自由能分析下简并的动力学通道。数值实验（Muller-Brown势、周期性景观、丙氨酸二肽）验证了其有效性，表明随机反应动力学存在传统热力学描述无法捕捉的额外几何层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学描述（如自由能垒、熵产生）对构型空间的局部几何结构不敏感，无法区分动力学上不同的反应通道，需要一种能捕捉局部几何信息的新框架。

Method: 从过阻尼Langevin动力学出发，将其表述为Wasserstein空间中的梯度流，推导出变分泛函的渐近结构，定义局部耗散-几何耦合可观测量，该量结合力-漂移对齐与漂移场散度引起的相空间压缩，反映能量景观的二阶几何特征。

Result: 该可观测量能够区分在自由能分析下简并的动力学通道，在Muller-Brown势、波纹周期势及丙氨酸二肽构象转变的数值实验中表现出稳健的路径分离能力，并在高频均匀化区域呈现二次标度行为。

Conclusion: 随机反应动力学中存在额外的一层几何动力学控制，这是标准热力学或反应坐标描述无法捕获的，本文提出的框架为揭示这一隐藏层次提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We propose a geometric framework for characterizing hidden kinetic constraints in stochastic reaction dynamics. While free-energy barriers and entropy production provide global descriptors of thermodynamic behavior, they are largely insensitive to local geometric structure in configuration space that governs pathway selection. Starting from overdamped Langevin dynamics formulated as a gradient flow in Wasserstein space, we derive a variational functional whose leading-order asymptotic structure defines a local dissipation-geometry coupling observable. This quantity combines force-drift alignment with phase-space contraction induced by the divergence of the drift field, yielding a scalar field that reflects second-order geometric features of the underlying energy landscape. We demonstrate that this observable distinguishes kinetically distinct reaction channels that are degenerate under conventional free-energy analysis, as shown through numerical experiments on benchmark systems including the Muller-Brown potential, corrugated periodic landscapes, and the conformational transitions of Alanine Dipeptide. These experiments demonstrate robust separation of pathways and are consistent with a quadratic scaling behavior in the high-frequency homogenization regime. Our results suggest that stochastic reaction dynamics contain an additional geometric layer of kinetic control in molecular motion not captured by standard thermodynamic or reaction-coordinate descriptions.

</details>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Phase diagram of magnetic $S^3$ Skyrmions on three-dimensional lattices and the toroidal antiSkyrmion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07740)
*Niccolò Francini,Stefano Bolognesi,Sven Bjarke Gudnason,Roberto Menta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维磁性斯格明子的推广：从$\mathbb{R}^3$到$S^3$的西格玛模型，采用两种SO(3)不变广义DMI（$\alpha$项和$\beta$项），通过蒙特卡洛模拟构建有限温相图，首次发现单位电荷的环形（反）孤子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二维磁性斯格明子（由DMI稳定的平面孤子）自然推广到三维空间，研究四分量磁化矢量在$S^3$流形上的拓扑磁结构及其热力学行为。

Method: 采用两种SO(3)不变广义DMI（$\alpha$项支持球对称 hedgehog 斯格明子，$\beta$项支持轴向对称斯格明子）；推导可与连续理论匹配的立方晶格离散化方案；使用蒙特卡洛模拟映射有限温相图。

Result: 识别出自旋螺旋相、磁弦晶格相、斯格明子晶格相、反斯格明子晶格相，以及$S^3$分数电荷分布于弦弯曲处的混合拓扑区域；首次发现单位电荷的环形（反）孤子。

Conclusion: 建立了$S^3$序参量流形三维拓扑磁结构的理论与计算框架，揭示了反禁闭（半斯格明子由负张力磁弦连接的分离现象）等新奇物理现象。

Abstract: Magnetic Skyrmions are planar solitons stabilized by the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) and realized in chiral magnets. We study their natural three-dimensional generalization: a sigma model from $\mathbb{R}^3$ to $S^3$ with a four-component magnetization vector, stabilized by a one-derivative term which is a generalized DMI. We utilize two SO(3)-invariant generalized DMIs discovered recently: an "$α$-term" supporting a spherically symmetric hedgehog Skyrmion and a "$β$-term" supporting an axially symmetric Skyrmion that splits into two half-Skyrmions connected by a magnetic string of negative tension, a phenomenon we call "anti-confinement". We derive a cubic-lattice discretization that reproduces both continuum theories at long wavelengths and use Monte Carlo simulations to map the finite-temperature phase diagram. We identify spin-spiral, magnetic-string-lattice, Skyrmion-lattice, and antiSkyrmion-lattice phases, as well as a mixed-topology regime with fractional $S^3$ charges localized at string bends. We find, for the first time in the literature to the best of our knowledge, a toroidal (anti-)soliton of unit charge. Our results establish a theoretical and computational framework for three-dimensional topological magnetic textures in systems whose order-parameter manifold is $S^3$.

</details>


### [108] [Detecting Exciton Condensation through Charge Transport in Semiconductor Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07752)
*Caterina Zerba,Léo Mangeolle,Michael Knap*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用掺杂载流子的电荷输运作为探测过渡金属硫族化合物异质结构中激子凝聚的实验探针，揭示了两种显著特征：凝聚导致电阻率降低，以及在可调固体Feshbach共振附近，凝聚诱导的掺杂载流子与三子束缚态杂化产生负有效质量并导致霍尔电阻率符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体异质结构中激子凝聚的直接实验证据仍然难以获取，现有探测手段存在局限性，亟需新的可测量实验信号来明确验证激子凝聚的存在。

Method: 通过理论分析掺杂载流子在激子凝聚体系中的电荷输运行为，考虑凝聚对载流子散射相空间的抑制效应，以及在可调固体Feshbach共振条件下掺杂载流子与三子束缚态的杂化耦合对输运性质的量子修正。

Result: 发现两个关键实验结果：(1) 激子凝聚通过抑制散射相空间降低体系电阻率，可作为一般性诊断信号；(2) 在Feshbach共振附近，凝聚诱导的杂化导致掺杂载流子有效质量为负，引起霍尔电阻率符号反转。

Conclusion: 电荷输运测量为探测和表征半导体异质结构中的激子凝聚提供了一条有前景的实验途径，上述特征信号可作为激子凝聚存在的直接实验证据。

Abstract: Direct evidence of exciton condensation in semiconductor heterostructures remains elusive. Here we propose charge transport of doped carriers as a probe of exciton condensation in transition-metal dichalcogenide heterostructures and identify distinct experimental signatures. First, condensation suppresses the phase space for carrier scattering, leading to a reduction in resistivity, that provides a general diagnostic of exciton condensation. Second, in heterostructures with a tunable solid-state Feshbach resonance, condensate-induced hybridization between doped carriers and trion bound states qualitatively modifies transport. In particular, near resonance, this hybridization yields a negative effective mass and a corresponding sign reversal of the Hall resistivity. These results establish charge transport as a promising route for detecting and characterizing exciton condensation in semiconductor heterostructures.

</details>


### [109] [Coherent control of chirality in Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07921)
*Amar Bharti,Margarita Khokhlova,Misha Ivanov,Gopal Dixit*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明了在反演对称Weyl半金属中，通过两色光场（圆偏振ω场与线偏振2ω场）的相干干涉，可以打破手性对称性并选择性激发单个Weyl节点，产生可控的非零光电流，且光电流的大小和符号可通过光的相对相位和场强调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反演对称Weyl半金属中Weyl费米子以相反手性成对出现，导致圆偏振光下的光学响应对称且净光电流为零，作者希望打破这种对称性以实现对手性电荷动力学的光学控制。

Method: 采用两色光场方案：圆偏振的基频ω场与相位锁定的线偏振倍频2ω场干涉，通过相对相位和场强调控，实现对手性依赖的载流子动量空间重分布，从而产生可控光电流。

Result: 成功实现了选择性激发单个Weyl节点，产生了非零且可控的光电流，发现光电流的大小和符号可通过两色光的相对相位和场强调节，并确定了手性选择性最大化的最优参数区域。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种利用偏振结构光在Weyl半金属中实现光学控制手性电荷动力学的通用方法。

Abstract: Weyl fermions in inversion-symmetric Weyl semimetals occur in pairs of opposite chirality, leading to symmetric optical responses under circularly-polarised light and a vanishing net photocurrent. Here, we show that tailored two-colour light fields break this symmetry and enable selective excitation of individual Weyl nodes. The interference between a circularly-polarised $ω$ field and a phase-locked linearly-polarised $2ω$ field generates a chirality-dependent redistribution of carriers in momentum space, resulting in a nonzero controllable photocurrent. We demonstrate that both the magnitude and sign of the photocurrent can be tuned via the relative phase and field strength of the two colours, and identify an optimal regime in which chiral selectivity is maximised. Our results establish a general route to optically-controlled chiral charge dynamics in Weyl semimetals using polarisation-structured light.

</details>


### [110] [Equilibrium spin currents in altermagnet junctions: Josephson-like and anomalous transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07983)
*Abhiram Soori*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了二维交错磁体异质结中的平衡自旋电流，发现AM-NM-AM结呈现类似约瑟夫森行为的自旋电流，而将中心层替换为p波磁体后，即使在奈尔矢量完全对齐时也能产生反常耗散自旋电流，该现象源于空间反演对称性破缺导致的量子相位不对称。临界自旋电流随能带填充呈现显著波动，归因于介观量子尺寸效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在零净宏观磁化的交错磁体材料中实现新奇自旋相关现象，特别是平衡自旋电流的产生机制及其调控可能性。

Method: 采用紧束缚晶格模型理论计算二维交错磁体异质结构，分别研究AM-NM-AM结和AM-PM-AM结，分析奈尔矢量相对角度对自旋电流的影响，以及空间反演对称性破缺带来的反常输运行为。

Result: AM-NM-AM结中σ_y极化平衡自旋电流表现为类约瑟夫森行为，由奈尔矢量夹角θ决定；AM-PM-AM结中即使θ=0也存在有限耗散自旋电流（反常平衡自旋电流）；临界自旋电流随带填充呈现由介观量子尺寸效应导致的波动。

Conclusion: 交错磁体异质结可用于设计无耗散、相位可调的自旋电子学器件，特别是通过p波磁体实现的反常平衡自旋电流为新型自旋输运机制提供了途径。

Abstract: Altermagnets (AMs) offer a compelling platform for exploring novel spin-dependent phenomena in materials with zero net macroscopic magnetization. In this work, we theoretically investigate the emergence of equilibrium spin currents (ESCs) in two-dimensional AM heterostructures using a tight-binding lattice model. We first study an AM-normal metal-AM (AM-NM-AM) junction and demonstrate that the $σ_y$-polarized ESC exhibits a characteristic Josephson-like behavior, fundamentally governed by the relative angle ($θ$) between the Néel vectors of the two AMs pointing in $xz$-plane. Crucially, we show that replacing the central normal metal with a $p$-wave magnet (PM) induces an anomalous ESC. Analogous to the anomalous Josephson effect, the breaking of spatial inversion symmetry by the PM allows a finite, dissipationless spin current to flow even when the Néel vectors are perfectly aligned ($θ=0$). We establish that this anomalous transport is driven by an asymmetry in the quantum phases accumulated by right- and left-moving electrons undergoing spin-flip reflections. Finally, we show that the critical ESC exhibits pronounced fluctuations as a function of band filling, which we attribute to mesoscopic quantum size effects, including transverse subband quantization and longitudinal Fabry-Pérot resonances. Our findings highlight the potential of altermagnet junctions for designing dissipationless, phase-tunable spintronic devices.

</details>


### [111] [DC conductivity of tilted Dirac Fermions across the Lifshitz Transition: short- versus long-range impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08010)
*Mohammad H. Pakzamir,Zahra Faraei,Ali G. Moghaddam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了倾斜狄拉克系统中直流电导率在短程和长程杂质散射下的行为，揭示了从亚临界到过临界倾斜区的输运特性差异、各向异性以及Lifshitz转变处的奇异性，并提出倾斜参数可作为调控输运的有效手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究倾斜狄拉克系统中DC电导率在不同杂质散射机制和不同倾斜相（亚临界、临界、过临界）下的行为规律，理解倾斜参数如何影响宏观输运性质。

Method: 基于Kubo形式论，分别考虑短程（点缺陷）和长程（库仑散射）杂质散射，系统研究亚临界（Type I）、临界（Lifshitz转变点，t=1）和过临界（Type II）三个倾斜区间的直流电导率，包括电导率的频率/能量依赖性、各向异性、顶点修正以及紫外截断效应。

Result: (1) 亚临界区：短程杂质给出单调递减的频率无关电导率；长程散射给出强能量依赖的电导率，但散射率与倾斜无关。(2) Lifshitz转变点：短程杂质出现电导率凹陷，长程杂质出现显著峰值。(3) 过临界区：需引入紫外截断；垂直于倾斜方向的电导率σ_xx对短程杂质在t=√2附近出现非单调峰，对长程杂质单调递减；沿倾斜方向的电导率σ_yy无界增长。(4) 顶点修正在Lifshitz转变点处对两类杂质均严格为零。(5) 长程杂质下，Type I和II的能量依赖分别接近二次和线性关系。

Conclusion: 建立了统一的几何框架，表明倾斜参数可作为调控狄拉克材料宏观输运的有力工具，揭示了不同倾斜相和杂质散射机制下的丰富输运行为。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the DC conductivity of two-dimensional tilted Dirac systems subject to short- and long-range impurity scattering. Using the Kubo formalism, we systematically study transport across the subcritical (Type I), critical, and overcritical (Type II) tilt regimes. In the subcritical phase, short-range impurities yield a frequency-independent conductivity that decreases monotonically with tilt. Conversely, long-range Coulomb scattering results in a strongly energy-dependent conductivity governed by a tilt-independent scattering rate. At the Lifshitz transition ($t = 1$), the transport signatures of these impurities diverge fundamentally: the van Hove singularity in the density of states induces a localized conductivity dip for short-range disorder, but a pronounced macroscopic peak for Coulomb impurities. In the overcritical regime, an ultraviolet momentum cutoff is required to regularize the open Fermi surface, leading to distinct behaviors for each impurity type. Notably, the conductivity perpendicular to the tilt direction ($σ_{xx}$) exhibits a cutoff-dependent, non-monotonic peak near $t = \sqrt{2}$ for short-range defects, while it decays monotonically with increasing tilt for long-range scattering. For both potentials, the conductivity along the tilt axis ($σ_{yy}$) increases without bound, revealing extreme transport anisotropy. For long-range impurities, the energy dependence of the conductivity becomes nearly quadratic and linear for Type I and II, respectively. Furthermore, vertex corrections vanish identically at the Lifshitz transition for both impurity types. Finally, we provide a unified geometric framework for these phenomena, establishing the tilt parameter as a powerful knob for engineering macroscopic transport in Dirac materials.

</details>


### [112] [Correlated $\mathcal{PT}$-Symmetric Antiferromagnetic Topological Insulators with Giant Nonlinear Anomalous Thermoelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08235)
*Heng-Yu Di,Zhen-Gang Zhu,Gang Su*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型，证明电子关联可以在反铁磁体中产生同时破缺空间反演对称性和时间反演对称性的$\mathcal{PT}$对称反铁磁拓扑绝缘体态，并揭示其高阶量子几何张量的奇异行为可通过热电性能、电导率和热导率的巨大增强来宏观观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索反铁磁体中电子关联驱动的拓扑相变，以及由此产生的新型拓扑物态和可观测的宏观输运现象。

Method: 采用扩展Kane-Mele-Hubbard模型，研究电子关联与自旋-轨道耦合的竞争，分析$\mathcal{PT}$对称反铁磁拓扑绝缘体态的拓扑性质和非线性响应。

Result: 发现电子关联可驱动$\mathcal{PT}$对称反铁磁拓扑绝缘体态，伴随能隙闭合引起的高阶量子几何张量奇异行为，并在宏观上表现为热电性能、电导率和热导率的显著增强。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了关联效应在反铁磁拓扑物态中的关键作用，并提出了通过机械应变和静电门控调控输运信号以实验探测关联拓扑材料的可行方案。

Abstract: Topological states in antiferromagnets (AFMs) offer a promising platform for exploring novel physical phenomena and advancing the applications of AFM spintronics. The AFM topological insulator (TI) state stands out as one of the most representative and prominent cases. Unlike the previously proposed AFM-TI states in noninteracting systems, here we employ an extended Kane-Mele-Hubbard model to demonstrate that electron correlations can give rise to a $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric AFM-TI state. This state breaks both spatial inversion symmetry $\mathcal{P}$ and time-reversal symmetry $\mathcal{T}$, and enables intrinsic topological nonlinear responses to dominate the leading-order dynamics of the system. The competition between electron correlations and spin-orbit coupling drives the system across a topological phase transition, where the closure of the bulk band gap induces singular behaviors in higher-order quantum geometric tensors. Such microscopic singular characteristics manifest macroscopically as pronounced enhancements in thermoelectric performance, charge conductivity, and thermal conductivity. These giant tunable transport signatures, which can be effectively modulated by mechanical strain and electrostatic gating, provide a feasible experimental route to probe and understand correlated topological materials.

</details>


### [113] [Simultaneous nanoscale imaging of local conductivity and chemical potential in a quantum Hall isospin ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08237)
*Jiawei Hu,Shiyu Zhu,Bohao Li,Yunhao Wang,Shuigang Xu,Zhihai Cheng,Chengmin Shen,Andre K. Geim,Fengcheng Wu,Hong-Jun Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文介绍了扫描电导率和化学势显微镜（SCCM），该技术集成扫描微波阻抗显微镜和开尔文探针力显微镜，能够同时绘制局域电导率和化学势图。通过研究双层石墨烯的量子霍尔态和多体朗道能级能谱，以及在轻微扭曲双双层石墨烯中发现的量子霍尔同位旋铁磁态级联及其复现行为，展示了SCCM在解码复杂量子现象方面的能力，建立了一种用于电子关联和拓扑研究的通用纳米级探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层石墨烯中的量子霍尔同位旋铁磁性为探索平带关联物理提供了丰富的平台，但缺乏能够同时绘制这些奇特相中局域电导率和化学势的纳米级探针。

Method: 提出扫描电导率和化学势显微镜（SCCM），集成扫描微波阻抗显微镜和开尔文探针力显微镜两种技术。

Result: 在双层石墨烯中成功探测了量子霍尔态和多体朗道能级能谱；在轻微扭曲双双层石墨烯中发现了量子霍尔同位旋铁磁态的级联及其意想不到的复现行为；揭示了这些复杂现象与子带间朗道能级交叉和朗道能级单粒子波函数的关联，构建了全面的量子霍尔相图。

Conclusion: SCCM能够解码复杂量子现象，是一种用于电子关联和拓扑研究的通用纳米级探针。

Abstract: Quantum Hall isospin ferromagnetism in multilayer graphene offers a versatile playground for exploring flat band correlated physics, driven by the intricate coupling of spin, valley, orbital, and layer degrees of freedom. However, a nanoscale probe capable of simultaneously mapping local conductivity and chemical potential in these exotic phases has yet to be realized. Here, we introduce scanning conductivity and chemical potential microscopy (SCCM), a technique integrating scanning microwave impedance microscopy and Kelvin probe force microscopy. We demonstrate SCCM by probing the quantum Hall states and many-body Landau level energy spectrum in bilayer graphene. Applied to marginally twisted double bilayer graphene, SCCM then reveals a cascade of quantum Hall isospin ferromagnetic states with unexpected re-emergence behaviors. Significantly, experimental many-body Landau level energy spectrum further uncovers the intricate connections of these complex phenomena to inter-subband Landau level crossings and Landau level single-particle wavefunctions. These insights enable the construction of a comprehensive quantum Hall phase diagram. Our results demonstrate SCCM's capability in decoding complex quantum phenomena, establishing it as a versatile nanoscale probe for electron correlation and topology.

</details>


### [114] [Fidelity susceptibility and geometric response in flux-tuned Dirac systems: exact results from a low-energy two-level reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08280)
*C. A. S. Almeida*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究二维大质量狄拉克费米子在阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆（Aharonov-Bohm）通量插入下的参数灵敏度，以Bures度量（保真度磁化率）作为统计力学响应函数。当约化通量接近整数时，低能谱发生通量诱导的避免交叉，其结构由狄拉克质量控制。通过将完整的狄拉克-AB算符低能投影到有效两能级子空间，推导出基态Bures度量的精确闭式表达式，呈现以整数通量为中心、宽度由质量参数决定的洛伦兹轮廓。峰值标度为g_max~m^{-2}，在手征极限下发散，类似于热力学磁化率在临界点附近的发散行为。引入的积分几何磁化率χ(m)=π/(8m)具有逆质量标度，是信息几何对应幂律临界行为的表现，其中狄拉克质量扮演相关耦合参数的角色。洛伦兹轮廓源于布洛赫球上基态流形的曲率，无需动力学输入。该几何响应独立于贝里曲率和拓扑不变量，源于普适的局域谱机制。通过谱表示，Bures度量被识别为持续电流磁化率的几何（顺磁）贡献，连接了信息几何与可测量的介观狄拉克系统响应函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维大质量狄拉克费米子在AB通量插入下的参数灵敏度，探索信息几何（Bures度量）在描述量子相变和临界行为中的应用，特别是与手征极限发散的类比，并建立与可观测物理量（持续电流磁化率）的联系。

Method: 使用Bures度量（保真度磁化率）作为核心响应函数；通过低能投影将完整的狄拉克-AB算符约化到有效两能级子空间；推导基态Bures度量的精确闭式解；利用Bloch球几何解释；通过谱表示将几何响应与持续电流磁化率关联。

Result: 基态Bures度量呈现普适的洛伦兹轮廓，峰值g_max~m^{-2}在手征极限(m→0)下发散；积分几何磁化率χ(m)=π/(8m)呈逆质量标度；该几何响应独立于贝里曲率和拓扑不变量，源于局域谱机制；Bures度量被识别为持续电流磁化率的几何（顺磁）贡献。

Conclusion: 狄拉克质量作为相关耦合参数控制距手征不动点的距离，信息几何响应提供了类似临界现象的标度行为；该几何机制普适且无需动力学输入，可直接连接介观狄拉克系统中的可测量响应函数。

Abstract: We study the parametric sensitivity of two-dimensional massive Dirac fermions subject to Aharonov-Bohm flux insertion, using the Bures metric (fidelity susceptibility) as the central statistical-mechanical response function. Near integer values of the reduced flux, the low-energy spectrum undergoes a flux-induced avoided crossing whose structure is controlled by the Dirac mass. Through a controlled low-energy projection of the full Dirac=Aharonov-Bohm operator onto an effective two-level subspace, valid near integer flux, we derive an exact closed-form expression for the ground-state Bures metric, which takes a universal Lorentzian profile centered at integer flux with width set by the mass parameter. The peak value scales as $g^{\max}_{λλ}\sim m^{-2}$, diverging in the chiral limit in direct analogy with the divergence of thermodynamic susceptibilities near critical points. We introduce an integrated geometric susceptibility $χ(m) = π/(8m)$, whose inverse-mass scaling is the information-geometric counterpart of power-law critical behavior, with the Dirac mass playing the role of a relevant coupling controlling the distance from the chiral fixed point. The Lorentzian profile is shown to arise from the curvature of the ground-state manifold on the Bloch sphere, requiring no dynamical input beyond the spectral structure. Importantly, this geometric response is independent of Berry curvature and topological invariants, emerging instead from a universal local spectral mechanism. Through its spectral representation, the Bures metric is identified as the geometric (paramagnetic) contribution to the persistent current susceptibility, encoding the sensitivity of persistent currents and orbital magnetization to flux variations and connecting information geometry to measurable response functions in mesoscopic Dirac systems.

</details>


### [115] [Topological bound states in the continuum with controllable multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08510)
*Ya-Ping Lou,Wei Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出在广义呼吸烧绿石晶格中实现角局域拓扑连续谱束缚态（BICs），通过调节胞间跳跃参数可任意控制每个角落的BIC数量，并利用极化拓扑荷进行精确表征，揭示了三种拓扑相变类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维系统中角局域拓扑BICs缺乏现实的理论模型和有效的拓扑表征方案，且如何可控地调节角局域拓扑BIC的数量仍是未解问题。

Method: 提出一类具有一般胞间跳跃的广义呼吸烧绿石晶格模型；通过调节胞间跳跃参数改变能带拓扑性质；利用极化拓扑荷（布里渊区中具有拓扑性质的节线点）表征角局域BICs。

Result: 在广义呼吸烧绿石晶格中观察到角局域拓扑BICs；每个角落的BIC数量可通过调节胞间跳跃参数任意调控；发现了与体能隙闭合方式相关的三种类型拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 这项工作促进了角局域拓扑BICs的理论研究，并为其未来实验观测开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) are spatially localized states embedded in the continuous spectrum without hybridizing with extended bulk modes. Recent advances in topological band theory have greatly enriched the understanding of BICs, which gives rise to boundary-localized topological BICs with extremely high robustness against disorders. However, there remains a challenge in realizing corner-localized topological BICs in a three-dimensional system due to the absence of both realistic theoretical models and effective topological characterization schemes. In particular, how to engineer a controllable number of corner-localized topological BIC is still an open question. Here, we propose that the corner-localized topological BICs can emerge in a class of generalized breathing pyrochlore lattice with general inter-cell hoppings. We further show that the number of BICs at each corner can be arbitrarily adjusted by changing the parameters of inter-cell hoppings. Remarkably, although these corner-localized topological BICs are intertwined with a substantial number of bulk modes, we can accurately characterize them through the polarized topological charges, which are nodal points with topological properties in Brillouin zone and are measurable in experiments. We also reveal three types of topological phase transitions of corner-localized BICs, which are associated with the different ways of closing the bulk energy gap and can be intuitively captured by the polarized topological charges. This work not only promotes the theoretical research of corner-localized topological BICs, but also opens an avenue for their experimental observation in the future.

</details>


### [116] [Novel 2D Altermagnetic Vanadium Oxide with a Buckled Lieb Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08637)
*Tamer Taşkıran,Soheil Ershadrad,Biplab Sanyal,Cüneyt Şahin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究通过第一性原理计算，发现单层V₂O晶体（翘曲Lieb晶格）是一种新型二维交变磁性材料，具有室温稳定性、负泊松比、面外磁各向异性、1.2 eV动量依赖自旋劈裂以及约40 (ℏ/e) S·cm⁻¹的本征自旋霍尔电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性（Altermagnetism）作为自旋电子学中新兴的有前途相，兼具反铁磁体和铁磁体的优势，但二维交变磁性材料仍有待发现和验证。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（DFT）的第一性原理分析，结合声子结构、从头算分子动力学（AIMD）和刚度矩阵验证稳定性，同时计算能带结构、自旋轨道耦合效应、贝里曲率和自旋霍尔电导率。

Result: V₂O形成能为负且结构/热力学稳定；具有负泊松比（辅助特性）；反铁磁基态，每个V原子局域磁矩2.79 μB；面外易磁化轴；能带中表现1.2 eV动量依赖自旋劈裂；自旋轨道耦合打开能带交叉，产生大贝里曲率和约40 (ℏ/e) S·cm⁻¹的自旋霍尔电导率。

Conclusion: 单层V₂O是一种稳健的室温交变磁性平台，兼具磁各向异性和自旋依赖输运响应，为自旋电子学应用提供了候选材料。

Abstract: Altermagnetism has recently emerged as a highly promising phase for spintronics, offering the combined advantages of both antiferromagnets and ferromagnets. Here, using a first-principles analysis based on density functional theory (DFT), we identify a monolayer V$_2$O crystal in a buckled Lieb lattice as a promising two-dimensional altermagnetic material. The structural and thermal stability of V$_2$O is verified through calculations of the crystal's formation energy, phonon structure, room-temperature ab initio molecular dynamics, and stiffness matrix. The system is found to exhibit auxetic behavior with a negative Poisson's ratio. Our calculations indicate an antiferromagnetic ground state with a local magnetic moment of $2.79\,μ_{\mathrm{B}}$ per V atom and a magnetocrystalline anisotropy that favors an out-of-plane easy axis. The electronic structure exhibits a momentum-dependent spin splitting of 1.2 eV, which is a characteristic of altermagnets. Inclusion of spin-orbit coupling breaks the symmetry of the quadratic band crossing near the Fermi level, resulting in a large Berry curvature and significant intrinsic spin Hall conductivity around $40\,(\hbar/e)\,\mathrm{S\,cm^{-1}}$. The results demonstrate that monolayer V$_2$O serves as a robust room-temperature altermagnetic platform, exhibiting magnetic anisotropy and spin-dependent transport responses.

</details>


### [117] [Topological invariant responsible for the integer QHE and non-commutative geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08868)
*G. Kovyrshin,A. Mekrami,J. Miller,M. A. Zubkov,A. Zuevsky*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过Wigner变换将二维紧束缚模型的拓扑不变量N₃（负责整数量子霍尔效应中霍尔电导的量子化）表示为K⁻¹群元素与循环上同调群HC³中特定元素的配对，并利用局部指标定理证明了该类模型N₃的整数值性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立二维（非均匀）紧束缚模型中拓扑不变量N₃的数学表达框架，并证明其在整数量子霍尔效应中的整数量子化性质。

Method: 采用Wigner变换将两电子Matsubara格林函数转化为相空间表示，将拓扑不变量N₃表述为K⁻¹群元素与循环上同调群HC³中特定元素的配对，并借助局部指标定理进行整数性证明。

Result: 成功将N₃不变量表达为K⁻¹群与HC³循环上同调群的配对形式，并针对一类受限紧束缚模型证明了N₃取整数值。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解非均匀紧束缚模型中整数量子霍尔效应的拓扑起源提供了严格的数学框架，将指标定理推广至更一般的晶格模型。

Abstract: We consider a wide class of $2D$ tight - binding models of solid state physics. These models are, in the most general case, non - homogeneous. The topological invariant ${\cal N}_3$ responsible for the quantization of the Hall conductivity, for the specific case of the integer quantum Hall effect in $2D$, is expressed through the Wigner transformation of the two-point electron Matsubara Green function. We express this invariant as a pairing of the element of the $K^{-1}$ group (generated by the Green function) with the specific element of the cyclic cohomology group $HC^3$. According to a set of local index theorems the values of ${\cal N}_3$ can be shown to be integer for a limited class of tight - binding models.

</details>


### [118] [Valley Engineering in Bilayer WSe$_2$ Gate-All-Around Transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08955)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi,Souren Adhikary,Kazuhito Tsukagoshi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了双层WSe₂中K-Γ谷分裂在室温下接近热能量子（k_BT）时，如何通过谷工程调控栅极全环绕场效应晶体管的输运性能，发现双层结构在零应变下即可实现最优的谷工程灵敏度，并能在保持亚阈值摆幅接近热离子极限的同时，通过压应变同时提升开态电流、抑制关态电流并改善开关比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示双层WSe₂中K-Γ谷近简并性（Δ_{KΓ} ≈ k_BT）对空穴输运的调控机制，探索能否通过谷工程而非传统迁移率工程来解耦开态性能与开关斜率。

Method: 结合自旋-轨道耦合的密度泛函理论（DFT）与解析双谷器件模型，分析不同层数及双轴应变下的输运特性。

Result: (i) 亚阈值摆幅受量子电容屏蔽保护，保持在~60 mV/dec，与层数无关；(ii) 有效迁移率由K-Γ谷占据比决定，随层数增加单调下降；(iii) 在双层中，压应变同时提升开态电流、抑制关态电流，开关比从~69提升至~156，亚阈值摆幅保持近热离子极限。

Conclusion: 谷工程灵敏度在Δ_{KΓ} ≈ k_BT时最大，该条件在室温零应变下的双层WSe₂中最自然满足，使其成为谷工程GAA晶体管的最优沟道材料。这建立了基于谷工程而非迁移率工程的设计原则，实现了开态性能与开关斜率的解耦。

Abstract: In bilayer WSe$_2$, interlayer coupling reduces the K--$Γ$ valley splitting to $Δ_{KΓ} \approx k_BT$ at room temperature, placing two hole-transport channels of markedly different effective mass in near-thermal equilibrium. We combine density functional theory (DFT) with spin--orbit coupling and an analytical two-valley device model to quantify how this near-degeneracy governs hole transport in gate-all-around (GAA) field-effect transistors. Three main results are obtained: (i)~the subthreshold swing is protected near $60$~mV~dec$^{-1}$ by quantum-capacitance screening independently of layer number; (ii)~the effective mobility is set by the K-to-$Γ$ valley occupation ratio and decreases monotonically with layer number; and (iii)~in the bilayer, compressive biaxial strain \emph{simultaneously} enhances the on-current, suppresses the off-current, and improves the on/off ratio from ${\approx}69$ to ${\approx}156$, while the subthreshold swing remains near the thermionic limit. This decoupling of on-state performance from switching slope is inaccessible through conventional mobility engineering and establishes a concrete design principle: \emph{valley-engineering sensitivity is maximized when $Δ_{KΓ} \approx k_BT$}, the condition most naturally satisfied by bilayer WSe$_2$ at room temperature and zero strain, making it the optimal channel for valley-engineered GAA transistors.

</details>


### [119] [Strain-Induced Tuning of Third-Harmonic Generation in Monolayer Black Phosphorene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09232)
*Yan Meng,Kainan Chang,Wei Song,Yuwei Shan,Jin Luo Cheng,Luxia Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于紧束缚模型和半导体布洛赫方程，系统揭示了应变工程调控单层黑磷烯三次谐波产生（THG）的微观机制。研究发现：无应变时单层黑磷烯具有显著面内各向异性，主导磁化率分量达1.8×10⁻¹⁷ m²/V²，与实验吻合；面内压应变和面外拉应变显著增强THG并导致红移，而面内拉应变和面外压应变抑制THG并导致蓝移，调控效率顺序为z > y > x；微观机制源于应变对带隙和Berry连接的协同调制；双轴应变的协同或竞争效应进一步丰富了THG信号调控手段。应变工程可作为动态调控二维材料非线性光学过程的有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示应变工程调控单层黑磷烯三次谐波产生的微观物理机制，探索利用应变作为动态调控二维材料非线性光学响应的有效手段。

Method: 基于紧束缚模型和半导体布洛赫方程，系统计算了单层黑磷烯在不同类型（单轴/双轴）和方向（x、y、z）应变下的三次谐波产生响应。

Result: 应变可有效调控THG：面内压缩和面外拉伸增强THG并产生红移；面内拉伸和面外压缩抑制THG并产生蓝移；调控效率顺序为z > y > x；双轴应变呈现协同或竞争效应。

Conclusion: 应变工程可作为动态调控二维材料非线性光学过程的策略，为开发可重构红外光子器件奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Based on the tight-binding model and the semiconductor Bloch equations, this work systematically reveals the microscopic mechanism of strain engineering in turning of third-harmonic generation (THG) in monolayer black phosphorene (BP). %
The results show that under strain-free conditions, monolayer BP exhibits significant in-plane anisotropy, and its dominant susceptibility component reaches a maximum of $χ^{(3);xxxx} = 1.8 \times 10^{-17} \, \text{m}^2/\text{V}^2$, agreeing well with the experimental results. %
By applying uniaxial and biaxial strains along the armchair ($x$), zigzag ($y$), and out-of-plane ($z$) directions, we find that the THG response presents strong direction dependence and unique spectral shifting behaviors: in-plane compressive strain and out-of-plane tensile strain both significantly enhance the THG conductivity and induce a redshift, whereas in-plane tensile strain and out-of-plane compression lead to suppression and a blueshift, with the tuning efficiency following the order of $z > y > x$. The microscopic origin of these phenomena is identified as the synergistic modulation of the bandgap and Berry connection by strain. %
Furthermore, the synergistic or competitive effects of biaxial strain further enrich the manipulation of THG signals. %
Strain engineering can serve as an effective strategy for dynamically controlling nonlinear optical processes in two-dimensional materials, and it also lays a theoretical foundation for the development of high-performance reconfigurable infrared photonic devices.

</details>


### [120] [Correlation enhanced resistance hysteresis near half filling in MoS2/WSe2 heterobilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09244)
*Yong Chen,Weikang Zhang,Meizhen Huang,Shengling Xiang,Zishu Zhou,Yaqi Ma,Chenxuan Lou,Haoxi Ji,Aoqian Zhang,Yifei Jin,Liheng An,Zefei Wu,Chun Cheng,Ning Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在MoS2/WSe2莫尔超晶格器件中观察到半填充附近的关联增强电阻迟滞现象，器件具有高场效应迁移率（17,650 cm²V⁻¹s⁻¹），分析表明位移场诱导的金属-绝缘体转变与界面偶极子耦合是迟滞的成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维材料及范德华异质结中新型铁电现象，特别是莫尔超晶格中的滑动铁电性和关联驱动铁电性。

Method: 制备MoS2/WSe2莫尔超晶格器件，通过电输运测量研究关联绝缘态及电阻迟滞行为，分析温度和位移场依赖关系。

Result: 器件场效应迁移率达17,650 cm²V⁻¹s⁻¹，在半填充处观察到关联绝缘态伴随显著且可重复的电阻迟滞，温度和位移场实验证实迟滞具有关联增强特性。

Conclusion: 关联增强的电阻迟滞源于位移场诱导的金属-绝缘体转变与界面偶极子的耦合，为探索新兴量子相和器件功能提供了机会。

Abstract: Ferroelectricity, typically arising from ionic displacements in noncentrosymmetric lattices, enabling applications in memory devices and sensors. Recent advances in two-dimensional materials and van der Waals heterostructures have revealed novel ferroelectric phenomena, including sliding ferroelectricity and correlation-driven ferroelectricity in moire superlattices. In this work, we fabricate and study a MoS2/WSe2 moire superlattice device exhibiting a high field-effect mobility of 17,650 $cm^2V^{-1}s^{-1}$. Electrical transport measurements reveal correlated insulating states accompanied by a prominent and reproducible resistance hysteresis near half filling. Temperature and displacement field dependence further confirms the correlation-enhanced nature of the hysteresis. Our analysis suggests that displacement field-induced metal-to-insulator transition at correlated insulating state coupled with interfacial dipoles enables the observed resistance hysteresis. These results establish correlation enhanced resistance hysteresis near half filling in a MoS2/WSe2 heterobilayer, offering opportunities for exploring emergent quantum phases and device functionalities.

</details>


### [121] [Non-Bloch band theory of boundary-controlled magnon edge modes in an antiferromagnetic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09267)
*Suman Debnath,Sonu Verma,Rohit Mukherjee,Arijit Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在非布洛赫能带理论框架下定义了绕数，成功解释了反铁磁自旋链中虽传统布洛赫绕数为零但仍出现的边缘模，并通过边界势调控实现了拓扑边缘态的可控转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统布洛赫能带理论下的体-边对应关系在反铁磁自旋链中失效——尽管动力学矩阵的对称性分类给出平庸的体态不变量，但有限系统仍表现出边界局域模。作者旨在解决这一体-边不匹配问题，并探索边界调控手段。

Method: 在线性自旋波理论框架下，将磁振子激发对应的动力学矩阵视为非厄米矩阵（尽管原始哈密顿量是厄米的），引入广义布里渊区和非布洛赫绕数来重新定义拓扑不变量。

Result: 非布洛赫绕数能正确预测边缘态的存在，而传统布洛赫绕数为零。边界势的调控可以将边缘模驱入或驱出体态谱，实现边界控制的拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 该工作在反铁磁范德瓦尔斯纳米结构中建立了通过局域塞曼场或修正的边缘各向异性即可实验观测的边界控制拓扑转变机制。

Abstract: We define a winding number within the Non-Bloch band theory framework that captures the emergence of magnon edge modes in a one-dimensional antiferromagnetic spin chain, even when the conventional Bloch winding number is trivial. Within linear spin-wave theory, magnon excitations are governed by a non-Hermitian dynamic matrix, despite the underlying Hamiltonian being Hermitian. The symmetry classification of this matrix yields a trivial bulk invariant, however, finite systems exhibit boundary-localized modes, signaling a breakdown of the conventional bulk-boundary correspondence. We further show that these edge modes can be controlled via boundary perturbations. By tuning the boundary potential, the modes can be driven into or out of the bulk spectrum. To resolve the bulk-boundary mismatch, we develop a non-Bloch framework based on a generalized Brillouin zone and a winding number that correctly predicts the presence of edge states. Our results establish boundary-controlled topological transitions that are experimentally accessible through local Zeeman fields or modified edge anisotropy in antiferromagnetic van der Waals nanostructures.

</details>


### [122] [Unconventional incommensurate epitaxy of superconducting FeSe films on SrTiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09281)
*M. Klement,K. M. Fijalkowski,M. Kamp,C. Gould,L. W. Molenkamp*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过XRD和STEM研究FeSe/FeTe多层膜/SrTiO₃(001)体系，发现FeSe与SrTiO₃界面存在方向对齐但原子无匹配的异常外延模式，即无失配位错的定向生长，为理解层状超导体中的应变调节和界面工程提供新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究FeSe/FeTe多层膜在SrTiO₃(001)衬底上的外延生长机制，尤其是界面处原子级匹配与晶格应变的关系，以理解非常规外延模式并优化铁基超导体的界面工程。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）在SrTiO₃(001)衬底上生长FeSe/FeTe多层膜，结合X射线衍射（XRD）和扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）对晶体结构和界面原子排布进行表征。

Result: XRD证实FeSe、FeTe与衬底之间具有完美的面内外延取向对齐；STEM显示FeSe/SrTiO₃界面缺乏原子级匹配，FeSe保持自身面内晶格间距而非适应衬底；观察到周期性的横向原子位错，其重复长度与XRD测得的晶格失配一致；界面处未检测到失配位错或其他弛豫特征。

Conclusion: FeSe/SrTiO₃体系展示了一种非常规外延机制：晶向定向保持但无需原子级匹配，即"无注册的外延"模式。这一发现为层状系统中的应变调节提供了新视角，对铁基超导体的界面工程具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We present a combined X-ray diffraction and transmission electron microscopy study of superconducting FeSe/FeTe multilayers grown by molecular beam epitaxy on SrTiO$_3$(001) substrates. While X-ray diffraction confirms perfect in-plane epitaxial alignment between FeSe, FeTe, and the substrate, scanning transmission electron microscopy reveals a surprising lack of atomic registry at the FeSe/SrTiO$_3$ interface. Instead of adapting to the substrate lattice, FeSe retains its own in-plane lattice spacing. A periodic lateral shift between the atomic positions of FeSe and SrTiO$_3$ is observed, with a registry recurrence length that matches the lattice mismatch determined by X-ray diffraction. No misfit dislocations or other relaxation features are detected at the interface. This coexistence of directional alignment and registry-free growth suggests an unconventional regime of epitaxy in which crystallographic orientation is maintained without atomic matching. The findings offer insight into strain accommodation in layered systems and may have implications for interface engineering in Fe-based superconductors.

</details>


### [123] [Planar Hall effect in single and bilayer Rashba systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09506)
*Rahul Biswas,Sunit Das,Amit Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有Rashba自旋轨道耦合的单层和双层二维电子气中的平面霍尔效应（PHE），识别出两种不同机制：塞曼耦合通道和能带几何通道。利用半经典玻尔兹曼输运理论计算了两种机制的平面霍尔电导率，发现主导贡献来自塞曼诱导效应，而能带几何通道在不对称Rashba双层中提供独特的对称性允许贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示具有Rashba自旋轨道耦合的二维电子气中平面霍尔效应的微观起源，识别并区分Zeeman耦合和能带几何两种不同机制，理解各向异性磁输运的物理本质。

Method: 采用半经典玻尔兹曼输运理论，对单层和双层Rashba自旋轨道耦合二维电子气中的平面霍尔效应进行理论分析；通过对称性分析确定响应的磁场二次方依赖和π周期角依赖性。

Result: Zeeman通道中，面内磁场畸变Rashba能带色散产生各向异性载流子速度，导致PHE；不对称Rashba双层中，层间电子离域产生有限平面贝里曲率和轨道磁矩，形成能带几何PHE通道。主导响应为磁场的二次方，呈π周期角依赖。在所考虑参数范围内，Zeeman诱导贡献占主导。

Conclusion: 揭示了自旋轨道耦合二维材料中各向异性磁输运的微观起源，区分了Zeeman耦合和能带几何两种PHE机制，指出能带几何通道是不对称Rashba双层特有的对称性允许贡献。

Abstract: The planar Hall effect (PHE) is an anisotropic magnetotransport response generated by coplanar electric and magnetic fields. We investigate the PHE in single- and bilayer two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs) with Rashba spin-orbit coupling and identify two distinct mechanisms: Zeeman coupling and a band geometric channel. In the Zeeman channel, an in-plane magnetic field distorts the Rashba spin-orbit-coupled band dispersion and generates anisotropic carrier velocities, producing a finite PHE. In an asymmetric Rashba bilayer, interlayer electronic delocalization generates finite planar Berry curvature and orbital magnetic moment components, giving rise to a band geometric PHE channel. Using semiclassical Boltzmann transport theory, we calculate the chemical potential and angular dependence of the planar Hall conductivity for both mechanisms. Symmetry analysis shows that the leading response is quadratic in the magnetic field and exhibits the characteristic $π$-periodic angular dependence. For the parameter regime considered here, the Zeeman-induced contribution dominates, while the band geometric channel provides a distinct symmetry-allowed contribution unique to asymmetric Rashba bilayers. Our results reveal microscopic origins of anisotropic magnetotransport in spin-orbit-coupled two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [124] [Frequency-resolved decoherence spectroscopy of a semiconductor charge qubit coupled to a high-impedance resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09722)
*Ekaterina Al-Tavil,Wonjin Jang,David J. van Woerkom,Ville Maisi,Stefano Bosco,Jan A. Krzywda,Jeroen Danon,Christian Reichl,Werner Wegscheider,Thomas Ihn,Klaus Ensslin,Andreas Wallraff,Pasquale Scarlino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究构建了GaAs双量子点电荷量子比特与高频可调SQUID阵列谐振器耦合的混合电路量子电动力学系统，通过频率分辨退相干光谱学在3-6 GHz范围内区分了电荷量子比特的弛豫与纯退相过程，发现低频段退相干主导、高频段能量弛豫主导，且弛豫率呈立方频率依赖，归因于压电声子耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究固态量子系统中光-物质相互作用和退相干机制，特别是对半导体超导混合量子器件中电荷量子比特的退相干过程进行频率分辨的精细表征。

Method: 构建GaAs双量子点电荷量子比特与高频阻抗、频率可调的SQUID阵列谐振器电容耦合的混合电路量子电动力学体系，通过调谐量子比特跃迁频率在3-6 GHz范围内进行频率分辨退相干光谱学测量，并结合时间分辨测量区分弛豫与纯退相过程。

Result: 在低频段（≤4.5 GHz）退相干主导线宽，高频段能量弛豫成为主要贡献；弛豫率随频率呈立方标度关系，符合电荷量子比特通过压电声子库耦合衰减的预期。

Conclusion: 混合半导体-超导电路可作为高灵敏度的光谱学工具，用于探测多种混合量子器件中微观退相干机制。

Abstract: Superconducting resonators coupled to semiconductor quantum dots provide a powerful platform to investigate light-matter interaction and decoherence mechanisms in solid-state quantum systems. Here we study a hybrid circuit quantum electrodynamics architecture consisting of a GaAs double-quantum-dot charge qubit capacitively coupled to a high-impedance, frequency-tunable SQUID-array resonator. By tuning the qubit transition frequency over the range $ω_\mathrm{q}/2π\sim 3$-$6$ GHz, we perform frequency-resolved decoherence spectroscopy of the charge qubit across a broad energy window. Time-resolved measurements enable us to disentangle relaxation and pure dephasing processes and to identify distinct decoherence regimes as a function of qubit frequency. We find that at lower frequencies ($\leq 4.5$ GHz) dephasing dominates the qubit linewidth, whereas at higher frequencies energy relaxation becomes the leading contribution. The measured frequency dependence of the relaxation rate exhibits a cubic scaling, consistent with charge-qubit decay dominated by coupling to a piezoelectric phonon bath and providing frequency-resolved access to the corresponding phonon-induced spectral density. Our results show that hybrid semiconductor--superconducting circuits can serve as sensitive spectroscopic tools to probe microscopic decoherence mechanisms relevant for a wide range of hybrid quantum devices.

</details>


### [125] [Radiowave-induced Resistance Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09755)
*E. Bell,C. Hnatovsky,K. W. Baldwin,L. N. Pfeiffer,K. W. West,S. Studenikin,M. A. Zudov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报告了一类新型辐射诱导磁阻振荡，在UHF频段、ω_c >> ω条件下产生，其振幅与辐射功率无关、周期由辐射电场控制，并呈现1/B或1/B²周期性，区别于传统MIRO，并在短程无序极限下通过位移模型得到解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIRO在ω_c ≲ ω条件下由单光子跃迁产生，而本文旨在探索非传统条件下（ω_c >> ω）的新型辐射诱导磁阻振荡现象，揭示其与MIRO的本质区别并建立理论解释。

Method: 实验观测UHF频段辐射下二维电子气中的磁阻振荡，系统分析振荡对辐射功率、磁场、辐射电场等参数的依赖关系；理论方面采用短程无序极限下的位移模型进行解释。

Result: 发现不同于MIRO的新型振荡：(i) 发生在ω_c >> ω条件下；(ii) 振幅不依赖辐射功率；(iii) 周期由辐射电场控制而非ω；(iv) 随B不同可表现为1/B或1/B²周期性。

Conclusion: 这类新型振荡在物理机制上完全不同于MIRO，可通过短程无序极限下的位移模型加以解释，拓展了对辐射诱导输运现象的理解。

Abstract: Microwave-induced resistance oscillations (MIROs) \cite{zudov:2001a} occur when a 2D electron gas is subjected to radiation of frequency $ω= 2 πf$ and varying magnetic field $B$. MIROs are periodic in $1/B$, with the period determined by the radiation frequency $ω$, and their amplitude scales with the radiation power. Stepping from single-photon transitions between Landau levels, MIROs are found on the lower-field side of the cyclotron resonance, $ω_c \lesssim ω$, where $ω_c$ is the cyclotron frequency. Here, we report on experimental observation of another class of magneto resistance oscillations, which are also induced by radiation, but in the radio frequency (UHF band) range. These oscillations are distinct from MIROs in the following aspects: (i) they occur at $ω_c \gg ω$, (ii) their amplitude is independent of radiation power, (iii) their period is controlled by the radiation electric field, rather than by $ω$, and (iv) they can be either $1/B$ or $1/B^2$-periodic, depending on $B$. We further show that these oscillations can be explained by a displacement model in the limit of short-range disorder.

</details>


### [126] [Persistent currents, whirlpools, and local Chern markers in twisted TMD Chern insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09812)
*Francesco Cioni,Lorenzo Cavicchi,Nazzareno Africani,Giacomo Mazza,Fabio Taddei,Amir Yacoby,Marco Polini*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭曲过渡金属硫族化物同质双层中的拓扑有序态，分析了自发时间反演对称性破缺产生的自旋极化涡旋电流，发现体相电流密度最大值可准确表征拓扑序，并计算了有限尺寸效应对霍尔电导量子化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲过渡金属硫族化物同质双层系统已被证实可承载整数和分数量子陈绝缘态，但其基态中时间反演对称性自发破缺产生的自旋极化涡旋电流尚未得到定量分析，需要建立电流与拓扑序之间的量化关系。

Method: 首先对莫尔尺度上的持续自旋极化电流进行定量分析，然后通过数值计算考察体相电流密度幅值最大值与拓扑序之间的关联，最后计算有限尺寸效应对霍尔电导量子化的影响。

Result: 体相中电流密度幅值的最大值可精确追踪拓扑有序态；有限尺寸效应会影响霍尔电导的量子化行为。

Conclusion: 体相电流密度最大值可作为拓扑序的有效探针，为实验上通过扫描探针方法检测拓扑态提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Recent materials advances have made it possible to fabricate twisted transition metal dichalcogenide homobilayers. These systems have been shown to host integer and fractional Chern insulating states. Because of spontaneous time reversal symmetry breaking, their ground state harbors intriguing spin-polarized currents with whirlpools on the moiré length scale that can be measured by scanning probe methods. We first provide a quantitative analysis of these persistent currents and then show that the maximum of the amplitude of the current density in the bulk of the sample is an accurate tracker of topological order. We conclude by calculating how the quantization of the Hall conductance is affected by finite-size effects.

</details>

### [127] [Length-resolved Operator Growth and Path-Entropy Obstructions to Many-Body Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07774)
*J. Sirker*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文证明无序Ising链中局域算子的时间演化展现几乎阶乘增长的算子权重空间离域化，排除了任意无序强度下的动力学局域化（最强形式的多体局域化），并建立了有限尺寸交叉标度律L~(W/J)²，同时指出路径熵障碍阻碍了微扰LIOM构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Cao关于无序Ising链中算子矩几乎阶乘增长的结果，通过解析算子范数在支撑长度上的分布，证明算子权重的最大空间离域化，并排除任意无序强度下的动力学局域化。

Method: 解析求解算子范数在支撑长度上的分布，建立有限尺寸交叉标度律，并识别路径熵障碍对微扰LIOM构造的阻碍机制。

Result: 在长度ℓ_k ~ k/ln k处，算子权重呈现几乎阶乘增长‖[H,σᶻ₀]⁽ᵏ⁾_ℓ_k‖₂ ≳ (k/ln k)^k；有限尺寸交叉标度L~(W/J)²；路径熵障碍表明亚弹道或局域化动力学需要目前未知的抵消原理。

Conclusion: 任意无序强度下多体局域化的最强形式（动力学局域性）被排除；数值研究在L≲(W/J)²时仅能访问预渐近区域；算子内容的几乎阶乘分支构成对微扰LIOM构造的结构性障碍。

Abstract: For the disordered Ising chain with transverse and longitudinal fields, where couplings and fields are drawn from strictly positive distributions, Cao~\cite{Cao} has shown that the moments $μ_{2k} = \|[H,σ^z_0]^{(k)}\|_2^2$ grow almost factorially, $μ_{2k}^{1/(2k)}\sim k/\ln k$, and thus asymptotically at the maximal allowed rate. We generalize this result by resolving the operator norm in support length and show that the weight at length $\ell_k \sim k/\ln k$ already exhibits almost factorial growth, $\|[H,σ^z_0]^{(k)}_{\ell_k}\|_2 \gtrsim (k/\ln k)^k$. This implies maximal spatial delocalization of local operators and, in particular, rules out dynamical locality -- the strongest form of many-body localization -- at any disorder strength. We further establish rigorously a finite-size crossover scale $L\sim (W/J)^2$, where $W$ is the disorder and $J$ the coupling strength. For $L\lesssim (W/J)^2$ numerical studies only access a pre-asymptotic regime. Finally, we identify a structural path-entropy obstruction to perturbative LIOM constructions, based on the almost factorial branching of operator content and independent of resonance effects; the same mechanism strongly suggests ballistic real-time operator spreading, so sub-ballistic or localized dynamics would require a presently unidentified cancellation principle acting on almost factorially many disorder-dependent paths with random amplitudes.

</details>


### [128] [Dynamical cavity method for continuous-time complex systems on sparse random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08689)
*Fernando L. Metz,Isaac Pérez Castillo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文为稀疏异质随机网络上的随机动力学发展了连续时间腔方法的动力学平均场理论（DMFT），在路径测度层面给出了精确的树图解和局部树状图上的有限时间热力学描述，明确了互惠性对动力学闭包的影响，并推导了高连接性极限下与标准稠密DMFT的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DMFT适用于稠密高维无序动力学，但对于稀疏异质网络，局域场仅包含有限个强输入，高斯闭包机制不再适用，亟需发展适用于稀疏网络的DMFT框架。

Method: 采用连续时间腔方法，在路径测度层面推导稀疏随机图上一般成对相互作用的随机动力学的DMFT。通过区分有向图和互惠（双向）边，导出不同的条件路径核。利用多重线性和分支独立性进行消息矩闭包，并给出因果离散时间版本的人群动力学表示。

Result: 在树图上获得精确腔方程，在局部树状图上得到有限时间热力学描述；有向图恢复稀疏有向路径概率方程，互惠图需要条件路径核；导出了人群动力学表示；在加性输入递归神经网络特例中测试了有限记忆数值闭包；获得了高连接极限下与标准低维DMFT的投影关系。

Conclusion: 稀疏网络DMFT通过腔方法严格建立，互惠性本质改变闭包结构，高连接极限下可还原为稠密DMFT但在某些场景下路径级描述仍不可或缺。

Abstract: Dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) reduces dense high-dimensional disordered dynamics to a self-consistent effective stochastic process. For sparse and heterogeneous networks, however, local fields contain finitely many strong inputs, so the Gaussian closure mechanisms of dense DMFT need not apply. We develop a continuous-time cavity derivation of sparse-network DMFT at the level of path measures for stochastic dynamics with general pairwise interactions on sparse random graphs. The cavity equations are exact on trees and yield the finite-time thermodynamic description on locally tree-like graphs. They show explicitly how reciprocity changes dynamical closure: fully directed graphs recover the sparse directed path-probability equation, whereas reciprocal or bidirected edges require conditional path kernels driven by the imposed history of the receiving node. Ensemble averaging gives laws over path-probability messages, with barycenters and higher-message moments closing by multilinearity and independence of incoming branches. A causal discrete-time derivation yields the corresponding population-dynamics representation, distinguishing trajectory populations for directed graphs from conditional branch-law or finite-depth tree populations for reciprocal graphs. We also formulate finite-memory numerical closures and test them in an additive-input recurrent neural network specialization. Finally, high-connectivity limits are obtained as projections of the sparse path-measure theory, clarifying when dense drift, noise, and response channels reduce to standard low-dimensional DMFT and when path-level descriptions remain essential.

</details>


### [129] [Statistical Mechanics of Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08706)
*Isaac Pérez Castillo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文是一篇基于2024年春季学院讲座的讲义，系统介绍了随机矩阵统计力学方法，重点涵盖稀疏与稀释随机矩阵的谱理论，包括空穴法、复制法、 resolvent 技术、群体动力学、典型谱密度、谱计数涨落与大偏差、条件谱以及非厄密推广等内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为复杂系统物理领域的研究者提供一套从统计力学视角理解稀疏与稀释随机矩阵谱理论的教学性系统论述，弥补传统教材在空穴法、复制法等统计力学工具与随机矩阵理论交叉领域的不足。

Method: 采用讲义形式，系统发展空穴法（cavity method）与复制法（replica method）的数学框架，结合 resolvent 技术、群体动力学（population dynamics）等计算工具，分析典型谱密度、谱计数涨落、大偏差以及条件谱等谱统计性质，并延伸至非厄密矩阵情形。

Result: 形成了一套结构化的教学材料，涵盖从基础方法到前沿应用（如非厄密推广）的完整知识体系，并在讲义中扩展了超出实际授课范围的补充内容以增强连贯性和前瞻性。

Conclusion: 该讲义为稀疏随机矩阵的统计力学分析提供了系统性的教学参考，虽选题偏向作者自身工作，但力求公平反映领域全貌，为未来更深入的讲座或学校课程指明了方向。

Abstract: These lecture notes are based on the lectures on \emph{Statistical Mechanics of Random Matrices} delivered at the Spring College on the Physics of Complex Systems, held at the Abdus Salam International Centre for Theoretical Physics, Trieste, Italy, from 19 February to 15 March 2024. Their aim is to present a statistical-mechanics route to the spectral theory of sparse and diluted random matrices, with emphasis on cavity and replica methods, resolvent techniques, population dynamics, typical spectral densities, spectral-count fluctuations and large deviations, conditioned spectra, and non-Hermitian extensions. The written form of the notes has been deliberately expanded beyond the material actually covered during the lectures. This is partly because a set of lecture notes can afford a more systematic development than a sequence of blackboard lectures, and partly because several natural continuations of the material become clearer once the central methods have been introduced. Consequently, not every topic discussed here was presented during the College. The additional material is included to give a more coherent account of the subject and to indicate directions that, hopefully, can be covered in greater detail in future lectures or schools. Since these are lecture notes rather than a state-of-the-art review, the choice of topics is necessarily selective and is naturally tilted towards the author's own work and collaborations on this subject. I have nevertheless tried, within the limits of this format, to place the material in contact with the broader literature and to represent the surrounding state of the art as fairly as possible. Inevitably, some relevant contributions may be missing or treated too briefly; such omissions are unintentional and reflect the pedagogical scope of the notes rather than a judgement on their importance.

</details>


### [130] [Predictable Mean-Field Chaos in Random Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08805)
*Alkesh Yadav,Vladimir Shaidurov,Jonathan Kadmon*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于具有足够快傅里叶衰减解析非线性的随机递归网络，其动力学平均场理论并非仅是一种系综描述，而是对单个轨迹的条件预测理论。通过将功率谱展开到Krylov状态空间，揭示了该潜在确定性在无限时间模态层级中的组织方式，并建立了Krylov增长率与有限分辨率预测复杂度及最大李雅普诺夫指数上界之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清动力学平均场理论中表观随机性的本质：证明在特定条件下，该随机性只是表象，平均场轨迹的连续过去唯一决定了其未来，从而将平均场理论提升为个体轨迹的条件预测理论。

Method: 利用解析非线性的傅里叶快速衰减条件，结合Krylov状态空间对功率谱进行展开，将平均场理论的潜在确定性组织为无限时间模态层级，并分析Krylov增长率与预测复杂度及李雅普诺夫指数之间的关系。

Result: 平均场理论中的随机性在解析非线性且傅里叶快速衰减条件下仅为表观现象；Krylov增长率刻画了有限分辨率预测的复杂度，并作为该类网络中最大李雅普诺夫指数的上界；微观敏感性与预测复杂度是平均场混沌的两个不同方面。

Conclusion: 将Krylov增长思想从哈密顿混沌动力学扩展到了经典耗散系统，为理解随机递归网络中的混沌行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: Dynamical mean-field theory recasts deterministic chaos in random recurrent networks as an effective stochastic process. We show that for analytic nonlinearities with sufficiently fast Fourier decay, this stochasticity is only apparent: the continuous past of a realized mean-field trajectory uniquely determines its future. The mean-field theory is therefore not merely an ensemble description, but a conditional prediction theory for individual trajectories. Unfolding the power spectrum into a Krylov state space exposes how this latent determinism is organized across an infinite hierarchy of temporal modes. The associated Krylov growth rate sets the complexity of finite-resolution prediction and upper-bounds the largest Lyapunov exponent in this class of networks. Thus, microscopic sensitivity and predictive complexity are distinct aspects of mean-field chaos. Our results extend Krylov growth ideas developed for Hamiltonian chaotic dynamics to classical dissipative systems.

</details>
